T4 developer handoff

0.2.0 · Agroforestry · Target country: Not selected

Defined means logic can be implemented; provisional means an input assumption remains; decision needed means response or scenario choices remain. Evidence references are separate. No state establishes validated spatial execution or target-country transfer.

One row per variable. Family-specific plots and inputs stay separate. Study use counts the recorded model-use cohort, not the whole library. Missing weights mean unknown. Source summaries are descriptive, not runtime weights.

Aggregation method

Descriptive medians: one median per reporting source across its alternative models, then across sources within the same model scope. Not independent-study validation or a runtime weighting recommendation. Group shares sum only mapped variables, without renormalization.

Relative importance compares source-model weights with equal weighting (1×). Model scopes remain separate. Mapped group shares may be partial; they are not declared group weights. Direction counts cover explicit direction fields only. Source IDs are not necessarily independent studies. Evidence-only variables have no executable mapping.

Plots show exact scored intervals, not fitted curves. Open circles exclude boundaries; filled circles include them. Arrows show unbounded tails truncated for display. Gaps and unscored intervals remain unknown.

distance_to_waterbody__global
  • Derive the stream network from upstream drainage area, then compute Euclidean distance to the network; threshold at the T4 band half-width. Derive-then-aggregate: never resample the distance raster from a coarser network.

Derive the stream network from MERIT upstream drainage area, then Euclidean distance to it. ~90 m: the 5-30 m bands the evidence specifies are below the network's meaningful resolution - scoping-grade only, and a national hydrography upload is preferable where one exists.

~90 m cell: cannot resolve the 5-30 m buffer bands the riparian evidence specifies, so a 30 m band is scoping-grade only. Small and intermittent streams are under-represented. Non-commercial licence clause. Not for: engineering-grade buffer delineation.

Recipe fallback
  • Reclassify to agricultural mask (cropland, grassland, sparse vegetation) to define eligible agroforestry conversion area. Exclude built-up, water bodies, bare soil with high salinity. Aggregate to 100m or 1km for pipeline. Used as LULC base by multiple AF studies.

Recipe fallback; no matching BIND row

Mixed-pixel confusion in fragmented agricultural landscapes. Annual snapshot — does not capture seasonal cultivation. Accuracy lower in arid landscapes and under cloud cover. Not for: Crop-type mapping; sub-field land use classification

coffee_arabica_distribution__global
  • Physical crop area in ha, 46 crop bands in published crop-code order. Select BIND band; coffee includes coff and rcof. Sum component physical areas, divide by geodesic pixel area in ha. Preserve nodata; reject fractions outside [0,1]. Read COG windows, never download full raster. All technologies already includes irrigated and rainfed; do not add them again.

Physical-area ha, all technologies; published crop order. Prefer accurate local commodity map; coarse allocation fallback accepted by Pete 2026-10-20.

Modelled allocation, not direct observation. ~5 arc-minute support cannot be sharpened by resampling. Crop fractions retain extent, not suitability strength. Cross-crop overlap must be checked before area totals.
